**Rolling window:** 2026-09-28 through 2026-10-04

## Best date plans

### 2026-10-03 — Third United Lenape / Lunaapeew Nations Pow Wow + Agi's Counter

**Third United Lenape / Lunaapeew Nations Pow Wow, then a meal.** Eastern European cafe at Agi's Counter. It is an 18-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=171+East+Dr%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=818+Franklin+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/third-united-lenape-lunaapeew-nations-pow-wow/2026-10-03/) · [Restaurant](https://www.agiscounter.com/)

### 2026-10-04 — Third United Lenape / Lunaapeew Nations Pow Wow + Leland Eating and Drinking House

### 2026-10-03 — John Carroll Kirby + Grimm Tavern

**John Carroll Kirby, then dinner.** Brewery tavern at Grimm Tavern. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.

### 2026-10-02 — Hupo + Fall for Dance Festival

## What to do this week

### 2026-09-28

#### [2026 Sukkot Celebration at Prospect Park](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/sukkot-celebration-in-prospect-park/)

12:00 PM · festival · Free · Prospect Park: Carousel Lawn North · Prospect Park · Brooklyn · Score 64.0  
A public Sukkot harvest celebration with a sukkah gathering, arts and crafts, refreshments, nature activities, and limited free carousel rides with RSVP.
**Timing:** Free carousel rides for youth under 18 and one guardian per group are listed for 12-2pm with RSVP.
**Practical note:** NWS forecast calls for rain before 2pm; much of the celebration is outdoors, so bring rain protection.
**Why now:** A current city permit identifies this event date.
**On-sale watch:** RSVP is requested for the free carousel-ride component.
[Official](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/sukkot-celebration-in-prospect-park/) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/sukkot-celebration-in-prospect-park/)

#### [Nothing to See Here](https://www.targetmargin.org/NonsenseLab/)

7:00 PM · theater · Tickets start at $20 · The Doxsee Theater · Sunset Park · Brooklyn · Score 64.0  
Saskia Lane curates a one-night evening of songs, sounds, improvisations, wrong turns, and audience-prompted nonsense at Target Margin Theater's Nonsense Lab.
**Timing:** One-night Nonsense Lab event on Monday, September 28.
**Practical note:** Experimental performance; expect a hybrid of music, theater, and improvisation rather than a conventional play.
**Why now:** One-night event on September 28 within the Nonsense Lab festival.
**Book soon:** One-night event in a small theater.
[Official](https://www.targetmargin.org/NonsenseLab/) · [Tickets / booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/282/production/1290636?performanceId=11866316) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://theskint.com/)

#### [NYC Resistor Public Craft Night](https://www.nycresistor.com/)

6:30 PM · workshop · Free · NYC Resistor · Gowanus · Brooklyn · Score 60.0  
Open craft night at the NYC Resistor hacker collective: bring a project, use the space, meet members. Free and open to the public, 18 and over.
**Timing:** Mondays and Thursdays 6:30-9:30pm. Free, but 18+ only.
**Practical note:** Fourth-floor walkup; confirm on Eventbrite since craft night is occasionally cancelled for private classes.
**Why now:** Runs twice a week year-round and is one of the few genuinely free weeknight drop-in activities.
**On-sale watch:** The organizer asks for Eventbrite RSVPs so attendees can receive updates.
[Official](https://www.nycresistor.com/) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/public-craft-night-mondays-and-thursdays-tickets-162729866411)

## Where to eat

A tighter, source-checked set: keep the useful restaurant/event cards, downgrade ordinary menus to restaurant listings, avoid treating April/May openings as new September debuts, and remove one duplicate NoHo happy-hour card.

Dining research: 2026-09-28T10:38:28+00:00

No NYC Michelin-star claims were retained. Eater’s El Califa de León note refers to the Mexico City original being the world’s first Michelin-starred taqueria in 2024, not to the NYC branch. Le Jardinier’s official text mentions Michelin recognition for sister restaurants, not a verified award claim for this NYC event; Michelin official pages were not checked beyond the supplied public evidence.

More restaurants — Le Rock

Restaurant-owned menu shows a full lunch service with relatively flexible order size: $7 bread and butter, $24 onion soup gratinée, $28 half-dozen oysters with chipolatas, $32 moules frites, and higher-ticket brasserie classics.

Price: Lunch items fetched include Bread & Butter $7, Onion Soup Gratinée $24, 1/2 dozen oysters with chipolatas $28, Moules Frites $32, Steak Frites $60  
Schedule: Opening hours listed Monday-Friday 11:00-21:00, Saturday 16:00-21:00; lunch menu presented on official menu page  
Conditions: Reclassify as restaurant, not menu: this is an ordinary current lunch menu with item prices on the official menu page, not a special promotion. Source: https://www.lerocknyc.com/menus/
